# de-employee-income-tax: figure and rule ledger

Guide: [de-employee-income-tax](../skills/international/germany/de-employee-income-tax.md). Accepted Guide SHA-256: `32b10213e3bc5d405ac89a1479b3a9ab07133a62320100c5788195a667764f3c`.
Repackaged from the completed [independent country review](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/blob/bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf/ledgers/germany-2026-evidence/independent-review/report.md) at `bceaf70083a8c8573ba8558a339ab11b1b4095bf`. The Guide bytes, all current ledger claims and all original-topic destinations are preserved. This is a packaging change; it does not claim a new legal review or accountant attestation.
Original published ledger SHA-256: `da8ce9343991d819822d421e0488a12d8446ee9cfc2dd6aa5a157ba50aae779f`. Full source captures, earlier versions, scripts and review chains remain in the original archive; none is added by this replacement. Source excerpts below retain their actual read dates. Short excerpts identify the previously reviewed operative unit; they do not replace its complete conditions.

| 068 | The taxable base LF[105, 105] |    - Basic health and care insurance (employee share; statutory health reduced by 4% where sick pay is covered). For most employees it is. | [de-employee-income-tax.HEALTH](#q-dc1d3812c0d184), [de-employee-income-tax.HEALTHCAP](#q-4dccd3960b0387). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 072 | The taxable base LF[109, 109] | 6. **Child allowances or Kindergeld (§ 31 EStG).** The tax office compares. | 082 | Deadlines and filing LF[124, 124] | \| Mandatory filers, own return \| By 31 July 2027 (seven months after year end, § 149(2) AO). 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so the deadline moves to Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | [de-employee-income-tax.AO149-2](#q-81c4cd1e990a8a), [de-employee-income-tax.AO108](#q-fe0cdc1a7333a8). | 083 | Deadlines and filing LF[125, 125] | \| Mandatory filers, prepared by a tax adviser \| By the last day of February of the second following year (§ 149(3) AO): 29 February 2028. | 086 | Penalties and interest LF[131, 131] | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. It must set it if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: by 29 February 2028) unless an extension was granted, the tax is nil, or the tax does not exceed the wage tax withheld and prepayments (§ 152(2) and (3) AO). Amount: 0.25% per month or part month of the tax less withheld tax and prepayments, at least EUR 25 per month, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). An employee due a refund is not hit by the mandatory surcharge. | [de-employee-income-tax.AO152-1](#q-6c9f5a73398add), [de-employee-income-tax.AO152-2](#q-9da9bdeb4d4023), [de-employee-income-tax.AO152-3](#q-163d253f675643), [de-employee-income-tax.AO152-5](#q-70b64d17e6e411), [de-employee-income-tax.AO152-10](#q-27428b64bc4ac7). | 087 | Penalties and interest LF[132, 132] | - **Interest on the balance (§ 233a AO).** Runs from 15 months after year end, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026, until the notice takes effect. 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same interest, at the earliest from the day the tax was paid. | [de-employee-income-tax.AO233a-1](#q-112103e0a9c2ae), [de-employee-income-tax.AO233a-2](#q-df5fd797174af3), [de-employee-income-tax.AO233a-3](#q-2cc9ce303a8a79), [de-employee-income-tax.AO238-1a](#q-02230f18845b30), [de-employee-income-tax.AO238-2](#q-bc488b96cb3efb). | 132 | Example 2: married couple, classes III and V, one child, splitting LF[190, 190] | Jonas earns EUR 72,000 (class III, 15 km on 200 days, so commuting is EUR 1,140 and the EUR 1,230 lump sum is higher). Mia earns EUR 18,000 (class V) and pays EUR 200 union dues; she has no other job costs. One child, Kindergeld EUR 3,108 for the year. Both in statutory health insurance at a 2.9% additional rate, both work outside Saxony (so care is 1.8% each), no church. Because one spouse was in class V, they must file (§ 46(2) no. 3a EStG). Wage tax withheld on both certificates together: EUR 9,414 (stipulated fictional certificate amounts: EUR 7,564 for Jonas and EUR 1,850 for Mia). | a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF88 | Work down this ladder. Each spouse computes their own employment income; the rest is joint under joint assessment (§ 26b EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF91, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF90 | 1. **Gross wages** from the wage tax certificate (Lohnsteuerbescheinigung), line 3. Tax-free items stay out. | Exact wording retained at current LF93, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF91 | 2. **Less Werbungskosten**, or the EUR 1,230 lump sum if higher. Common items: | Exact wording retained at current LF94, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF92 |    - **Commuting:** full km of the shortest road distance × EUR 0.38 × days at the first place of work. Public transport costs above the allowance count instead (§ 9(2) EStG). Tax-free employer travel benefits reduce it. | Exact wording retained at current LF95, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF93 |    - **Home office:** EUR 6 for each day worked mainly at home where the employee did not go to the first place of work. | a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF94 |    - **Work equipment:** laptop, tools, typical work clothing; items over EUR 800 net are depreciated over their useful life. | Exact wording retained at current LF97, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF95 |    - **Training** for the current or a future job, after a first qualification (§ 9(6) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF98, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF96 |    - **Union dues:** from 2026 deducted in addition to the lump sum (§ 9a sentence 3 EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF99, The taxable base  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF97 |    - Business travel, removal for work, second household, job application costs, account fees for salary. | Exact wording retained at current LF100, The taxable base  |

| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF106 | 6. **Child allowances or Kindergeld (§ 31 EStG).** The tax office compares. If the tax saving from the allowances is greater than the year's Kindergeld, it deducts the allowances and adds the Kindergeld to the tax. Otherwise Kindergeld stands and no allowance is deducted. The allowances are always used for the solidarity surcharge and church tax base (§ 3(2) SolzG, § 51a(2) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF109, The taxable base  |

| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF121 | \| Mandatory filers, own return \| By 31 July 2027 (seven months after year end, § 149(2) AO). 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so the deadline moves to Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF124, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF122 | \| Mandatory filers, prepared by a tax adviser \| By the last day of February of the second following year (§ 149(3) AO): 29 February 2028. The tax office can call for an earlier return (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained at current LF125, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF123 | \| Voluntary filers \| Four-year assessment period (§ 169(2) no. 2 AO), starting at the end of 2026 because there is no duty to file (§ 170(1) AO): the 2026 return must reach the tax office by 31 December 2030. The ELSTER 2025 guide states the same rule for 2025 (31 December 2029) and says it cannot be extended \| | Exact wording retained at current LF126, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF124 | \| Payment \| An extra payment is due one month after the notice (§ 36(4) EStG). The tax office may also set quarterly prepayments for later years \| | Exact wording retained at current LF127, Deadlines and filing  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF128 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. It must set it if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: by 29 February 2028) unless an extension was granted, the tax is nil, or the tax does not exceed the wage tax withheld and prepayments (§ 152(2) and (3) AO). Amount: 0.25% per month or part month of the tax less withheld tax and prepayments, at least EUR 25 per month, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). An employee due a refund is not hit by the mandatory surcharge. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Penalties and interest  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF129 | - **Interest on the balance (§ 233a AO).** Runs from 15 months after year end, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026, until the notice takes effect. 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same interest, at the earliest from the day the tax was paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Penalties and interest  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF130 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Penalties and interest  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF134 | - **Foreign pay of a resident.** Pay exempt in Germany under a tax treaty is still declared (Anlage N-AUS) and raises the rate on the rest (§ 32b EStG). Foreign tax credits under § 34c EStG are not covered here. | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF135 | - **Moving in or out in 2026.** Months of limited liability and German-exempt foreign income are handled under § 2(7) and § 32b EStG; the pack has no method yet. For shares and departure rules see leaving-germany-exit-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF138, Cross-border points  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF136 | - **Spouse living abroad.** An EU/EEA national may get joint assessment with a spouse resident in the EU/EEA under § 1a EStG; filing is then mandatory (§ 46(2) no. 7 EStG). Not covered further. | Exact wording retained at current LF139, Cross-border points  |
| a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF137 | - **Non-residents with German pay** (§ 1(3) election, § 46(2) no. 9 EStG) and Swiss cross-border commuters: refuse here. | a7faa477e5e2360544aab4240739d968d27b1195 LF187 | Jonas earns EUR 72,000 (class III, 15 km on 200 days, so commuting is EUR 1,140 and the EUR 1,230 lump sum is higher). Mia earns EUR 18,000 (class V) and pays EUR 200 union dues; she has no other job costs. One child, Kindergeld EUR 3,108 for the year. Both in statutory health insurance at a 2.9% additional rate, both work outside Saxony (so care is 1.8% each), no church. Because one spouse was in class V, they must file (§ 46(2) no. 3a EStG). Wage tax withheld on both certificates together: EUR 9,414 (invented case; worked out with the § 39b(2) EStG formula for these facts: class III EUR 7,564, class V EUR 1,850 at the 14% minimum; a payroll run can differ by a few euros). | Exact wording retained at current LF190, Example 2: married couple, classes III and V, one child, splitting Original certificate amounts retained as stipulated inputs; earlier false explanation as computed2026PAP corrected at currentLF190; original return algebra exact. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF24 | - **de-capital-gains**: investment income and the flat tax (Anlage KAP). | Exact wording retained at current LF24, Germany: income tax return for employees, tax year 2026 (Anlage N)  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF31 | - **In scope.** A person with a residence or habitual abode in Germany is fully liable to income tax on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG). This skill covers such a person whose income is mainly employment income (§ 19 EStG), with or without a spouse, with or without children. | Exact wording retained at current LF31, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF32 | - **Married couples and registered partners.** They may choose joint assessment (Zusammenveranlagung) if both are fully liable, not permanently separated, and met both conditions at some point in the year (§ 26(1) EStG). If neither chooses, joint assessment applies (§ 26(3) EStG). | Exact wording retained at current LF32, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF33 | - **Out of scope.** Non-residents with German wages (limited liability, § 1(4) EStG, or the § 1(3) election): use de-non-resident-tax. Self-employed, trade, capital and rental income go to the sibling skills listed above, then the results come back into this return. | Exact wording retained at current LF33, Who this applies to  |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF131 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** Only for mandatory filers. The tax office may set it after a late return. It must set it if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: by 29 February 2028) unless an extension was granted, the tax is nil, or the tax does not exceed the wage tax withheld and prepayments (§ 152(2) and (3) AO). Amount: 0.25% per month or part month of the tax less withheld tax and prepayments, at least EUR 25 per month, at most EUR 25,000 (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). An employee due a refund is not hit by the mandatory surcharge. | Exact wording retained at current LF131, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF132 | - **Interest on the balance (§ 233a AO).** Runs from 15 months after year end, so from 1 April 2028 for 2026, until the notice takes effect. 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same interest, at the earliest from the day the tax was paid. | Exact wording retained at current LF132, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF133 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. | Exact wording retained at current LF133, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF137 | - **Foreign pay of a resident.** Pay exempt in Germany under a tax treaty is still declared (Anlage N-AUS) and raises the rate on the rest (§ 32b EStG). Foreign tax credits under § 34c EStG are not covered here. | Exact wording retained at current LF137, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF138 | - **Moving in or out in 2026.** Months of limited liability and German-exempt foreign income are handled under § 2(7) and § 32b EStG; the pack has no method yet. For shares and departure rules see leaving-germany-exit-tax. | Exact wording retained at current LF138, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF139 | - **Spouse living abroad.** An EU/EEA national may get joint assessment with a spouse resident in the EU/EEA under § 1a EStG; filing is then mandatory (§ 46(2) no. 7 EStG). Not covered further. | Exact wording retained at current LF139, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF140 | - **Non-residents with German pay** (§ 1(3) election, § 46(2) no. 9 EStG): use de-non-resident-tax. Swiss cross-border commuters: refuse here. The commuter case belongs in germany-to-switzerland-cross-border-tax, which does not yet have its commuter part. | Exact wording retained at current LF140, Cross-border points  |
